([lucidIT-LLC/o-matic-firm](https://github.com/lucidIT-LLC/o-matic-firm/tree/HEAD/firm/skills/smith-evals))
- **smith-explore**: Pre-change exploration — audits codebase for system-wide impacts, identifies touch points, and surfaces conflicts before proceeding with smith-new or other workflows. ([ATTCKDigital/smith](https://github.com/ATTCKDigital/smith/tree/HEAD/skills/smith-explore) · ★55)
- **smith-failing**: Using std::failing (import std::failing; failing::make, failing::Failing): an allocator for tests that refuses some allocations by a draw from a random::Key, so a test reaches every allocation-failure path and a failure replays from its seed. Load when code imports std::failing or includes &lt;smith/failing.h&gt;, or on testing what a function does when memory is refused. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-failing))
- **smith-files**: Using std::files (import std::files; files::read_file, write_file, open, read, write, close, info, list, make_folder, remove, move, map, files::fake): files and folders by direct calls that return a result, with an in-memory fake for tests. Load when code imports std::files or includes &lt;smith/files.h&gt;, or on reading or writing a file. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-files))
- **smith-finish**: End-of-session workflow that commits, pushes, merges to main, updates specs, and cleans up the workspace. ([ATTCKDigital/smith](https://github.com/ATTCKDigital/smith/tree/HEAD/skills/smith-finish) · ★55)
- **smith-format**: Using std::format (import std::format; format::write, format::push, format::length, format::hex, format::fixed, format::general, format::scientific, format::pad, format::left, format::bytes): a type-checked formatter in place of snprintf, the pieces of the text as arguments. Load when a .smith file imports std::format, when a build.c calls smith_library(b, "format"), on writing text into a buffer or an arena in .smith, on replacing snprintf or printf, or on 'no rule for the type of an argument'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-format))
- **smith-gh-cli**: GitHub CLI (gh) gotchas. Use when running any gh command or querying GitHub from the terminal. ([tianjianjiang/smith](https://github.com/tianjianjiang/smith/tree/HEAD/smith-gh-cli) · ★1)
- **smith-gh-pr**: GitHub pull request workflows. Use when creating, updating, reviewing or merging a pull request (PR), replying to review comments, or working on stacked PRs. ([tianjianjiang/smith](https://github.com/tianjianjiang/smith/tree/HEAD/smith-gh-pr) · ★1)
- **smith-git**: Git workflow gotchas. Use when committing, branching, merging, rebasing, pushing or running raw git worktree commands. ([tianjianjiang/smith](https://github.com/tianjianjiang/smith/tree/HEAD/smith-git) · ★1)
- **smith-guidance**: Core agent steering. Always active. Use for every task, especially before an external write, a disagreement with the user, or a decision request. ([tianjianjiang/smith](https://github.com/tianjianjiang/smith/tree/HEAD/smith-guidance) · ★1)
- **smith-help**: Central reference for all Smith commands — overview, detailed help per command, syntax, flags, and workflow context. ([ATTCKDigital/smith](https://github.com/ATTCKDigital/smith/tree/HEAD/skills/smith-help) · ★55)
- **smith-http**: smith's HTTPS client, &lt;smith/http.h&gt; and lib/http.c: smith_http_get downloads one URL to a file on std::net and std::files (redirects to https only, streamed, idle timeout, errors that name the URL), what it does not do, and testing it on the net and files fakes. Load when code calls smith_http_get or includes &lt;smith/http.h&gt;, on a fetch or package download error such as 'short body' or 'HTTP 404', or when asked how smith downloads. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-http))
- **smith-ide**: IDE path variable mappings. Use when editing IDE config files (.vscode/, .kiro/, .cursor/) or translating path variables between IDEs. ([tianjianjiang/smith](https://github.com/tianjianjiang/smith/tree/HEAD/smith-ide) · ★1)
- **smith-idiom**: Idiomatic Smith: how to write, edit or review a .smith file so it is not C renamed (sized types, import &lt;h&gt;; with c::, modules, generics instead of sizeof, no preprocessor). Load when you write or edit a .smith file, or on 'idiomatic', 'C renamed', smith-sized-types or smith-c23 findings. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-idiom))
- **smith-idiom-after**: The rolls module as idiomatic Smith: the module, its main, and how each C habit became sized types, c:: names, arena::push and contracts. Load when porting C to .smith or reviewing a port. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-idiom-after))
- **smith-idiom-before**: A C module before porting, the rolls example: its header and source, with the habits that make a port C renamed. Load when reading C you are about to turn into a .smith module. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-idiom-before))
- **smith-idiom-port**: The porting checklist from C to idiomatic Smith, step by step with what to count before and after. Load when porting a .c file to .smith or when a reviewed .smith file still has uint32_t, size_t, #include or #define. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-idiom-port))
- **smith-implement**: Execute the implementation plan by processing and executing all tasks defined in tasks.md ([ATTCKDigital/smith](https://github.com/ATTCKDigital/smith/tree/HEAD/skills/smith-implement) · ★55)
- **smith-index**: Build and maintain the project manifest under .smith/index/. Full rebuild scans every source file, runs language parsers, and writes per-file .meta, per-system manifests, and a top-level summary. Supports --check (hash-only staleness), --system (partial rebuild), --incremental (git-diff scope), --describe (per-file LLM descriptions via Task sub-agents — subscription billing), --migrate-templates (constitution.md / CLAUDE.md), --init-system-paths, and --resume. ([ATTCKDigital/smith](https://github.com/ATTCKDigital/smith/tree/HEAD/skills/smith-index) · ★55)
- **smith-inflate**: Using std::inflate (import std::inflate): DEFLATE raw, zlib and gzip in memory with checksums. Load when code imports std::inflate or includes &lt;smith/inflate.h&gt;, or on decompressing gzip, zlib, PNG or HDF5 data in .smith or C without zlib. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-inflate))
- **smith-inflate-sink**: Streaming inflate to a sink for any size, and std::inflate from C (#include &lt;smith/inflate.h&gt;). Load when decompressing data bigger than memory or a .tar.gz, or from C. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-inflate-sink))
- **smith-json**: Reading JSON with smith's json (import std::json; &lt;smith/json.h&gt;): a tree from malloc and from C. Load for JSON, code that calls smith_json_ or includes &lt;smith/json.h&gt;, tokenizer.json, MCP, DAP or JSON-RPC messages. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-json))
- **smith-json-pull-write**: The pull parser and the bounded writer of smith's json: reading without a tree and writing a document to a fixed buffer. Load when parsing a large or streamed JSON or producing JSON. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-json-pull-write))
- **smith-lang**: The .smith language: C with modules, a superset of C by opt-in steps, and which skill teaches each step. Load when a file ends in .smith, on `module`, `import` or `export` in C source, smith cc --smith-header or --smith-lower, or 'needs a smith whose clang has the token hook'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang))
- **smith-lang-affine**: [[std::affine]] on a type: a value of it is used at most once, and a second use is a compile error naming the first (a key that must not be drawn from twice, a handle that must not be closed twice). Load when a type is marked [[std::affine]], or on 'smith-affine:', 'is used again; it was already used at line', 'is used in a loop, so it would be used once per iteration' or 'an element of an array of affine values is used through a constant index'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-affine))
- **smith-lang-array-dims**: More dimensions in .smith: `F32 xs[:][2]`, `xs[:][:]`, `I32 c[:][3][:]` (up to 8, one contiguous row-major block), a fixed array converting, a row `xs[i]`, `xs[i][a:b]`, `xs[a:b]` of rows, countof of each dimension. Load when a module has two `[:]` or a `[:]` before a number, or on 'a slice of rows has no strides', 'the array has not the shape of the slice'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-array-dims))
- **smith-lang-array-params**: Array parameters in a .smith module: `I32 xs[4]` keeps its size (checked indexes, countof(xs), `xs[static 4]` for clang and smith prove), `F32 m[3][2]`, why `I32 xs[]` and `sizeof(xs)` are errors, `char **argv` for main. Load when a module's parameter is written as an array, or on 'array parameter', 'an array parameter needs its size', 'pass an array of that shape', 'is not a pointer: it cannot be changed'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-array-params))
- **smith-lang-arrays**: Run-time arrays in .smith: `I32 xs[:]`, a slice (pointer and count) in a parameter, result, local, global or field; `countof(xs)`, `xs[i]`, `xs[a:b]`, `p[a:b]`, `char s[:] = "abc"`, `typeof(I32[:])`, checked in debug and release, and a slice passed to a generic. Load when a module has `[:]` or `xs[a:b]`, or on 'a slice has no step', 'an index is never negative', 'a pointer is no slice', 'cannot slice this expression'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-arrays))
- **smith-lang-c-headers**: A smith module seen from C and C++: the generated header (.smith/include/game/replay.h, extern "C", game_replay_open and its __asm__ label), what the header keeps (comments, #if lines, export inline), what it includes, calling a module from C or C++ and linking with smith c++. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-c-headers))
- **smith-lang-c-interop**: C into .smith: `import &lt;math.h&gt;;`, `c::printf`, `c::EOF`, `c::WIFEXITED(s)`, `struct c::stat`, `extern` for a symbol no header declares. Load on 'is a C name; write c::', 'is a C macro', 'names a C tag', `#include` in a .smith file, or `import "h";`. Calling a smith module from C or C++ is smith-lang-c-headers. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-c-interop))
- **smith-lang-concepts**: Concepts in .smith: a question of a type (Int, Float, Signed, Unsigned, Num, Pointer, Enum, Struct, Array, Slice) written where `auto` goes (`Num x`, `Int n = 3;`), so a wrong argument is an error at the call, and the same names as functions in `if constexpr (Int(T))`. Load when a parameter or local is written with one of those names, or on 'is not `Num`', 'is not `Int`' and the like. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-concepts))
- **smith-lang-constexpr**: Ordinary C functions run at compile time in a .smith file: constexpr i32 x = fib(10);, a lookup table built by a function, static_assert on a constexpr, case labels, array bounds and static initializers, the step limit, the depth limit and undefined behaviour as errors. Load when a .smith file computes a constant with a function, or on 'must be initialized by a constant expression', 'constexpr evaluation exceeded maximum depth', 'exceeded maximum steps' or 'in call to'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-constexpr))
- **smith-lang-constexpr-modules**: Compile-time calls across modules and failed contracts: an exported function of an import runs where C requires a constant (static_assert, constexpr, array bounds, if constexpr, a generic's result type), the .body file, its limits, and a failed contract::requires inside a compile-time call as a compile error with the call stack. Load when a constexpr calls an imported function, or on 'requires failed in call to'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-constexpr-modules))
- **smith-lang-defer**: defer in .smith modules: `defer mem::set_allocator(previous);` runs a statement when its block exits, last deferred first, on end of block, return, break, continue and goto out. Load when cleanup must run on every exit of a block (restore a handler, close a file, unlock, reset an arena), or on '`defer` is a keyword in a module'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-defer))
- **smith-lang-effects**: Effects in .smith modules and C: what a function does to the world (alloc, files, net, console, clock, process, env, entropy, globals, unknown, fails, code, tasks) is inferred from its body and its callees, [[std::pure]] promises nothing at all, [[std::effects(alloc, net)]] is a ceiling, and a call that breaks the promise is a compile error that names the chain. Load when marking a function [[std::pure]] or [[std::effects(...)]], or on 'smith-effect:', 'is [[std::pure]], but it has the effects' or 'is limited to'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-effects))
- **smith-lang-enums**: Enums with payloads in .smith: `typedef enum { Circle circle; Rect rect; empty; } Shape;`, picking a variant with a leading dot (`.empty`, `{.circle = {.radius = 1}}`), `s == .circle`, `switch` with no default, and reading `s.circle` only where the tag was tested (narrowing). Load when a .smith file declares an enum with a type and a name inside, or on `smith-enum:`, 'needs a type to look in', 'carries a payload', 'has no variant', 'is read where ... may not be a'. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-enums))
- **smith-lang-exports**: What a module's `export` takes and how an export is named in C: exported functions, typedefs, tags, constants, globals; the symbol game::replay::open and the C name game_replay_open; [[std::c_name("X")]], export inline, extern with and without export, a body defined in C. Load when writing `export`, [[std::c_name]], `export inline` or `extern` in a module, or on a C name clash or an `export static` error. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-exports))
- **smith-lang-for**: The range `for` of .smith: `for (T x : xs)` and `for (Usize i, T x : xs)` over a slice, a cut or a fixed array, `T *p` to write through, `auto` and concepts, and `for constexpr (auto f : fields(T))` over a compile-time list. - **smith-lang-generics**: Generic functions in .smith modules: `constexpr Type T` parameters (`arena::push(&a, Point)`), `constexpr Usize i` (a number known at compile time), `auto` parameters and `typeof(x)` (`check_equal(auto a, typeof(a) b)`), instances, the errors at a call. Load when a parameter list has `constexpr Type T` or `auto x`, on `typeof(x)`, or on 'in instance ..., requested from here', 'is called without its type', 'a type parameter is compile-time', 'is a generic function: it can only be called'. - **smith-lang-generics-auto**: How `auto` reads its type in a generic and what converts to it: the argument's exact compiler type (any expression), `cannot tell the type of`, and the conversion rule for later arguments (a value-changing conversion is an error at the call: `the argument 300 of check_equal converts to U8`), literals judged by value. Load on 'cannot tell the type of', 'converts to ..., the type of its first argument', or when an argument of a generic has the wrong type. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-generics-auto))
- **smith-lang-generics-more**: The rest of generics in .smith: `constexpr auto f` (a function passed by name, a direct call), a computed result type (`slice_of(T, countof(rest)) f(...)`), nested calls, private names in an exported generic's body, C and C++ cannot call a generic, lowering without a compiler. Load on 'is not a function: the parameter', a generic that takes a function, or a generic whose result type is computed. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-generics-more))
- **smith-lang-if-constexpr**: `if constexpr` in .smith: in a generic's body it chooses the branch for each instance by the type (typeof(value) == f32, is_integer, sizeof, is_slice, static_assert(false) in a final else); in any other function it chooses by the target. - **smith-lang-lambda-param**: `Lambda(F)`, the parameter type for code to run (`export void sort(auto xs[:], Lambda(Bool (typeof(xs[0]) *, typeof(xs[0]) *)) less)`): a lambda or a plain function as an argument, one instance each, F naming earlier parameters' types. - **smith-lang-lambdas**: Lambdas in .smith that capture nothing: `[](I32 a) -&gt; I32 { return a * 2; }`, `[](I32 a) { ... }` and `[] { ... }`, an expression that is a plain function and converts to a function pointer (qsort, a callback, a table). - **smith-lang-modules-errors**: The errors of modules and imports in .smith, each with its fix: 'import cycle', 'cannot find module', 'is private; export it', 'must be in a file whose path ends in', 'imports come before any declaration', 'found `x.h`, which is a C header', 'the header of module lists no export', 'needs a `module` line', 'cannot be imported'. - **smith-lang-reflection**: Reflection on a struct in a generic's body: `fields(T)` and `fields(typeof(E))` (a compile-time list of the named fields), `countof(fields(T))`, `fields(T)[i].name`, `x.[fields(T)[i]]`, `x-&gt;[...]` and `typeof(T.[...])`, so one function prints, compares or visits any struct. - **smith-lang-reflection-field**: `fields(T)[i]` as a `Field` value in .smith: `constexpr Field f = fields(T)[i];`, `f.name`, `f.type`, `is_float(f.type)`, a function over a Field, `x-&gt;[f]`, and slice and array fields (`countof(x.[f])`, `x.[f][i]`, `x.[f][a:b]`, `typeof(x.[f][0])`). - **smith-lang-rest**: The arguments of a generic and the loop over them: `auto... rest` (`rest...`, `rest[i]`, `countof(rest)`), `for constexpr (auto x : rest)`, `for constexpr (Usize i = 0; i &lt; countof(rest); i++)`, a type-checked loop by `typeof(rest[i])`, and why `auto args = {...}` (a built list) is gone. - **smith-lang-sized-types**: The built-in sized types of .smith files: u8 u16 u32 u64, i8 i16 i32 i64, usize, isize, f16 f32 f64, and bool. - **smith-lang-slice-pointers**: A pointer to a slice as C's pointer to an array: `const char (*text)[:]` (`*text` is the slice, `(*text)[i]`, `(*text)[a:b]`, `countof(*text)`, `*text = (*text)[n:]`), `typeof(const char[:]) *text`, a typedef'd `Text *text`, passing `&s`, a cursor such as `take_line(&s, &line)`. - **smith-lang-slice-qualifiers**: Qualifiers on a slice as C has them on a pointer: `const U8 xs[:]` (read-only elements), `U8 xs[const :]` (the slice itself fixed), `U8 xs[restrict :]` (no other pointer overlaps), `const U8 src[const restrict :]`, const slices in generics and in more dimensions. ([smith-ai-lab/smith-back](https://github.com/smith-ai-lab/smith-back/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/smith-lang-slice-qualifiers))
- **smith-lang-target**: The target and if constexpr in .smith, which has no preprocessor: import target; target::arch, os, assertions, address_sanitizer and the rest, what replaces #if and #define, and the error a #define gets.
